# baselines/e587b235/probes/substrate_to_double_range.txt
# ldc2 1.40.0 at gdtk e587b235, -O0, via probes/substrate/probe_to_double_range.d
# (5,309 rows: 160 real GridPro tokens + a 1..25-digit generated sweep + boundaries +
# midpoint hazards; two runs byte-identical, no addresses in any row).
#
# NOT REGISTERED AS A GATE. This is the measurement backlog T-930 asked for before anything
# is replicated, and the measurement says a replica is not available by transcription:
#   * over all 128,346 numeric tokens of examples/eilmer/3D/gridpro-import/blk.tmp, glibc
#     strtod differs from `to!double' on 15 of them (0.0117%), all 15-16 significant digits;
#   * to!double(s) == cast(double)(to!real(s)) on 201/201 sampled tokens, so the loss happens
#     in Phobos' 80-bit decimal->binary parse, not in the narrowing;
#   * the closest simple replica (exact integer mantissa x a long double binary-powered 10^e,
#     narrowed) differs from D on 3 of those 128,346 tokens — it fixes 13 but breaks 1
#     (`1.001179321978101': D and strtod both 3ff004d49bd53d05, the model 3ff004d49bd53d06);
#   * exactly rounding the decimal to 80-bit first (double rounding) explains 11/15;
#   * for inputs longer than 19 digits Phobos evidently leaves the machine-word path
#     altogether (the sweep rows with 20..25 digits do not fit any of the six simple models).
# So full fidelity needs Phobos' parseFloat/real internals, which are not source-available on
# this machine (ldc2 ships .di plus the compiled library) and cannot be fitted from these
# rows. Decision recorded in reports/backlog.md T-930.
